    # <details>
    # <summary>Release notes</summary>
    # <p><em>Sourced from <a href="https://github.com/LukasKalbertodt/litrs/releases">litrs's releases</a>.</em></p>
    # <blockquote>
    # <h2>v1.0.1</h2>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Fix parsing of (byte/C) strings starting with <code>\&lt;newline&gt;</code> (without other escapes). In those cases, the backslash, newline and whitespace were incorrectly kept.</li>
    # <li>Fix <code>to_compile_error</code> causing a second, unrelated error in item position. Output changed from <code>compile_error!(&quot;...&quot;)</code> to  <code>compile_error! { &quot;...&quot; }</code>.</li>
    # <li>Fix bug parsing <code>\u{}</code> (empty unicode escape). This resulted in a null byte, now it's correctly rejected.</li>
    # <li>Fix panic bug when parsing (byte/C) string literals with escapes and a trailing multi-byte char (e.g. <code>&quot;\n&quot;é</code>). These are super fringe cases as they require a literal suffix or a malformed literal.</li>
    # <li>Fix off-by-one bug regarding number of <code>#</code> in raw string literals. Before, 256 were &quot;allowed&quot;, which is wrong as Rust only allows up to 255. This also led to an overflow cast <code>as u8</code> which resulted in an incorrect <code>value()</code>.</li>
    # <li>Some documentation &amp; error message fixes</li>
    # </ul>
    # </blockquote>
    # </details>
    # <details>
    # <summary>Changelog</summary>
    # <p><em>Sourced from <a href="https://github.com/LukasKalbertodt/litrs/blob/main/CHANGELOG.md">litrs's changelog</a>.</em></p>
    # <blockquote>
    # <h2>[1.0.1] - 2026-10-12</h2>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Fix parsing of (byte/C) strings starting with <code>\&lt;newline&gt;</code> (without other escapes). In those cases, the backslash, newline and whitespace were incorrectly kept.</li>
    # <li>Fix <code>to_compile_error</code> causing a second, unrelated error in item position. Output changed from <code>compile_error!(&quot;...&quot;)</code> to  <code>compile_error! { &quot;...&quot; }</code>.</li>
    # <li>Fix bug parsing <code>\u{}</code> (empty unicode escape). This resulted in a null byte, now it's correctly rejected.</li>
    # <li>Fix panic bug when parsing (byte/C) string literals with escapes and a trailing multi-byte char (e.g. <code>&quot;\n&quot;é</code>). These are super fringe cases as they require a literal suffix or a malformed literal.</li>
    # <li>Fix off-by-one bug regarding number of <code>#</code> in raw string literals. Before, 256 were &quot;allowed&quot;, which is wrong as Rust only allows up to 255. This also led to an overflow cast <code>as u8</code> which resulted in an incorrect <code>value()</code>.</li>
    # <li>Some documentation &amp; error message fixes</li>
    # </ul>
    # </blockquote>
    # </details>
    # <details>
    # <summary>Commits</summary>

    # <li><a href="https://github.com/LukasKalbertodt/litrs/commit/85fba3eda47d394bf327fc9de8569b6fe65975af"><code>85fba3e</code></a> Minor doc and error message fixes</li>
    # <li><a href="https://github.com/LukasKalbertodt/litrs/commit/2e29c1bec12d19a476a7e15b7eafe5018c729e31"><code>2e29c1b</code></a> Improve <code>to_compile_error</code> output in item position</li>
    # <li><a href="https://github.com/LukasKalbertodt/litrs/commit/ae7a18d52a3ff5b11ad28ecd959e3e86924f93fb"><code>ae7a18d</code></a> Fix bug parsing <code>\u{}</code> (empty unicode escape)</li>
    # <li><a href="https://github.com/LukasKalbertodt/litrs/commit/67694b28afe4775475bf0205443cba22bad26ce4"><code>67694b2</code></a> Fix parsing of string starting with <code>\\&lt;newline&gt;</code> (without other escapes)</li>

    ^ /!\ contains changes to WPT expectations! it probably affects the web platform
    # A synchronous XMLHttpRequest blocked the event loop while waiting for the fetch response, so the timer scheduled for the request's timeout could never fire and the request always completed normally.
    # 
    # Added `ScriptEventLoopReceiver::recv_deadline` and used it in the synchronous fetch loop when a timeout was set, with the deadline being the start of the fetch plus the timeout. If the deadline passed before the response was processed, the fetch controller was terminated, the timeout error steps were run and a `TimeoutError` was thrown.
    #

    # Adds an optimization to style-driven animations (CSS animations and transitions), that avoids taking an expensive contended RwLock (`DocumentAnimationSet::sets`) where it is known that an element cannot have style-driven animations. .https://github.com/servo/servo/pull/49148	(@nicoburns, #49148)	layout: Fix quadratic blowup in flexbox wrapping code (#49148)
    perf; less work
    ^ commit 45239034aa3ab13be922de93a5349ea20c996163
    # Fix quadratic blowup in Flexbox wrapping code. The issue was in the use of `Vec::split_off` which allocates a new vector *for the tail* every time it's used. The new code still allocates a `Vec` for each line, but avoids reallocating a `Vec` for every remaining item (which can often be a much larger number than the number of items in the line.
    # 
    # Intuition: If you have 12000 items total and you segment it into 120 lines of 100 items each, we want to allocate 120 `Vec`s of size 100 (which this PR does). In the previous code we were allocating `Vec`s of size 11900, 11800, 11700, etc.
    # 
    # ### Benchmarks
    # 
    # | items | before fix | after fix |
    #  |---:|---:|---:|
    #  | 100 | 0.3 ms | 0.2 ms |
    #  | 1,000 | 14.7 ms | 1.6 ms |
    #  | 3,000 | 141.8 ms | 4.6 ms |
    #  | 12,000 | 2,039 ms | 20.4 ms |
    # 
    # Testing: WPT tests
    #

.https://github.com/servo/servo/pull/48590	(@mxmgorin, #48590)	webgl: Validate texStorage against its own targets and limits (#48590)
    wip; webgl
    ^ commit 95610d1e41077c313efc5954e55720652a86e307
    # This makes `texStorage2D` and `texStorage3D` validate their arguments according to the OpenGL ES 3.0 spec ([3.8.4](https://registry.khronos.org/OpenGL/specs/es/3.0/es_spec_3.0.pdf#page=149)):
    # 
    # * `texStorage3D` accepts `TEXTURE_3D` and `TEXTURE_2D_ARRAY` (previously returned `INVALID_ENUM`).
    # * `texStorage2D` accepts non-power-of-two sizes (previously returned `INVALID_VALUE`).
    # * Both generate `INVALID_OPERATION` on a texture that already has immutable storage (previously `texStorage2D` crashed the script thread).
    # * Compressed formats are rejected for `TEXTURE_3D`, as they are not supported for 3D textures ([3.8.6](https://registry.khronos.org/OpenGL/specs/es/3.0/es_spec_3.0.pdf#page=160)).
    # 
    # The texture binding and internal format checks are moved into helpers shared with the `texImage2D` and `texImage3D` validators.
    # This fixes Ruffle's wgpu renderer, which allocates non-power-of-two textures with `texStorage2D`, and Unity games that use texture arrays.
    # 
    # Testing: Three `conformance2/textures/misc/` tests now pass (`generate-mipmap-with-large-base-level`, `tex-3d-mipmap-levels-intel-bug`, `tex-storage-compressed-formats`), and many more subtests pass in other `conformance2` tests.
    #

    ^ commit 15cee107c1221287dc5d49bda7e2e265fdba6f46
    # Bumps [aws-lc-rs](https://github.com/aws/aws-lc-rs) from 1.18.1 to 1.18.2.
    # <details>
    # <summary>Release notes</summary>
    # <p><em>Sourced from <a href="https://github.com/aws/aws-lc-rs/releases">aws-lc-rs's releases</a>.</em></p>
    # <blockquote>
    # <h2>aws-lc-rs v1.18.2</h2>
    # <h2>What's Changed</h2>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Implement <code>Eq</code> and <code>PartialEq</code> for RSA and EdDSA parameters by <a href="https://github.com/dsykes16"><code>@​dsykes16</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/aws/aws-lc-rs/pull/1228">aws/aws-lc-rs#1228</a>
    # <ul>
    # <li><code>RsaParameters</code> can now be compared directly against RSA verification constants. Algorithms with different digests, padding schemes, or modulus-size ranges compare unequal. <code>EdDSAParameters</code> also gains equality support.</li>
    # </ul>
    # </li>
    # <li>Add <code>wasm32-wasip2</code> and <code>wasm32-wasip3</code> support by <a href="https://github.com/ricochet"><code>@​ricochet</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/aws/aws-lc-rs/pull/1239">aws/aws-lc-rs#1239</a>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Adds non-FIPS builds using universal bindings, with CI coverage under Wasmtime and wasi-sdk 34. WASIp3 requires nightly Rust.</li>
    # <li>Direct <code>aws-lc-sys</code> consumers must disable the default <code>all-bindings</code> feature for these targets.</li>
    # </ul>
    # </li>
    # <li>Publish the intended <code>RandomizedNonceKey::open_in_place</code> documentation by <a href="https://github.com/Serophots"><code>@​Serophots</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/aws/aws-lc-rs/pull/1227">aws/aws-lc-rs#1227</a>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Makes the existing nonce, ciphertext/tag layout, and buffer-on-error contract visible in rustdoc. No runtime behavior changes.</li>
    # </ul>
    # </li>
    # </ul>
    # <h3>Upstream AWS-LC</h3>
    # <ul>
    # <li><code>aws-lc-sys</code> v0.46.0 aligns with <a href="https://github.com/aws/aws-lc/releases/tag/v5.11.0">AWS-LC v5.11.0</a> (previously v5.7.0). See also the release notes for <a href="https://github.com/aws/aws-lc/releases/tag/v5.8.0">v5.8.0</a>, <a href="https://github.com/aws/aws-lc/releases/tag/v5.9.0">v5.9.0</a>, and <a href="https://github.com/aws/aws-lc/releases/tag/v5.10.0">v5.10.0</a>.
    # <ul>
    # <li>The Ed25519 C-implementation decoder now rejects non-canonical public keys, matching the assembly-optimized implementations: <a href="https://redirect.github.com/aws/aws-lc/pull/3457">aws/aws-lc#3457</a></li>
    # <li>Includes ChaCha20-Poly1305 key-material zeroization and fixes ML-KEM CPU feature detection to avoid illegal-instruction failures on x86-64 CPUs with AVX2 but no BMI2: <a href="https://redirect.github.com/aws/aws-lc/pull/3552">aws/aws-lc#3552</a> and <a href="https://redirect.github.com/aws/aws-lc/pull/3596">aws/aws-lc#3596</a></li>
    # </ul>
    # </li>
    # <li><code>aws-lc-fips-sys</code> v0.14.3 updates its bundled AWS-LC FIPS 4.2.0 snapshot and regenerates bindings. It remains on the FIPS 4.x release line.</li>
    # </ul>
    # <h3>Build Improvements</h3>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Raise the minimum system AWS-LC version to 5.7.0 by <a href="https://github.com/justsmth"><code>@​justsmth</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/aws/aws-lc-rs/pull/1243">aws/aws-lc-rs#1243</a>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Non-FIPS system-library installations must now provide AWS-LC 5.7.0 or newer, previously 1.68.0. Bundled-source builds and FIPS version requirements are unchanged.</li>
    # </ul>
    # </li>
    # <li>Fix static FIPS system-library linking by <a href="https://github.com/justsmth"><code>@​justsmth</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/aws/aws-lc-rs/pull/1236">aws/aws-lc-rs#1236</a>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Fixes unresolved <code>FIPS_mode</code> references with GNU ld.bfd when linking a prebuilt static FIPS library, including binaries that do not call crypto and builds where LTO removes those calls.</li>
    # <li>Uses whole-archive linking to retain libcrypto, including in downstream Rust <code>staticlib</code> outputs. This can increase binary size; dynamic FIPS and non-FIPS linking are unchanged.</li>
    # </ul>
    # </li>
    # <li>Fix Cranelift linking by <a href="https://github.com/justsmth"><code>@​justsmth</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/aws/aws-lc-rs/pull/1234">aws/aws-lc-rs#1234</a>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Normalizes binding link names to avoid undefined-symbol failures, including when Cranelift is selected through Cargo profiles rather than <code>RUSTFLAGS</code>. The <code>AWS_LC_SYS_NO_U1_BINDINGS</code> workaround is no longer needed.</li>
    # </ul>
    # </li>
    # <li>Fix jitterentropy builds with optimizing <code>CFLAGS</code> and newer <code>cc</code> versions by <a href="https://github.com/justsmth"><code>@​justsmth</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/aws/aws-lc-rs/pull/1254">aws/aws-lc-rs#1254</a>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Keeps filtered flags in effect throughout the jitterentropy builder's lifetime, preventing cached environment reads from allowing flags such as <code>-O2</code> to override its required <code>-O0</code>.</li>
    # </ul>
    # </li>
    # <li>Honor <code>AWS_LC_SYS_CC</code> in the main library build by <a href="https://github.com/justsmth"><code>@​justsmth</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/aws/aws-lc-rs/pull/1255">aws/aws-lc-rs#1255</a>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Applies crate-specific compiler overrides before compiler lookup in both CC and CMake build paths, keeping compiler selection and capability probes consistent when <code>cc</code> caches environment reads.</li>
    # </ul>
    # </li>
    # <li>Improve archive reproducibility through symlinked build paths by <a href="https://github.com/justsmth"><code>@​justsmth</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/aws/aws-lc-rs/pull/1238">aws/aws-lc-rs#1238</a>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Prevents the non-Windows CC builder from incorporating symlink-resolved absolute build paths into archive member names, avoiding unnecessary downstream compile-cache misses. Windows short-path handling is unchanged.</li>
    # </ul>
    # </li>
    # <li>Upgrade bindgen to 0.73 by <a href="https://github.com/xtqqczze"><code>@​xtqqczze</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/aws/aws-lc-rs/pull/1244">aws/aws-lc-rs#1244</a></li>
    # <li>Document the system-library trust model by <a href="https://github.com/justsmth"><code>@​justsmth</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/aws/aws-lc-rs/pull/1218">aws/aws-lc-rs#1218</a>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Clarifies that supplied paths and pkg-config results are trusted build inputs, and that FIPS validation can execute candidate library code before accepting or rejecting it. Documents runner requirements and how to disable automatic system-library detection.</li>
    # </ul>
    # </li>
    # </ul>
    # <h3>Issues Being Closed</h3>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Cranelift builds fail with <code>codegen-backend = &quot;cranelift&quot;</code> -- <a href="https://redirect.github.com/aws/aws-lc-rs/issues/1231">aws/aws-lc-rs#1231</a></li>
    # <li>Upgrade bindgen to v0.73 -- <a href="https://redirect.github.com/aws/aws-lc-rs/issues/1245">aws/aws-lc-rs#1245</a></li>
    # <li>Undefined <code>FIPS_mode</code> when linking a prebuilt FIPS module into a binary that does not use crypto -- <a href="https://redirect.github.com/aws/aws-lc-rs/issues/1246">aws/aws-lc-rs#1246</a></li>
    # <li>Jitterentropy fails with &quot;must not be compiled with optimizations&quot; in aws-lc-rs v1.18.1 / aws-lc-sys v0.45.0 -- <a href="https://redirect.github.com/aws/aws-lc-rs/issues/1252">aws/aws-lc-rs#1252</a></li>
    # </ul>
    # <h2>Other Merged PRs</h2>

    # <li>docs: align pull request template with AWS-LC by <a href="https://github.com/justsmth"><code>@​justsmth</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/aws/aws-lc-rs/pull/1219">aws/aws-lc-rs#1219</a></li>

.https://github.com/servo/servo/pull/49189	(@mrobinson, #49189)	script: Do not borrow `ResizeObservers` mutably when gathering (#49189)
    stability; part of https://github.com/servo/servo/issues/40600
    ^ commit 040e2840d4caca878d3b344c0981cf27a079935c
    # Previously this code was doing an unnecessary `borrow_mut()` while
    # calling into code that did layout (and thus could garbage collection).
    # This change addresses this in two ways:
    # 
    # 1. Clone the resize observer list as is done in other places that can
    #    garbage collect. This is better than simple `borrow()` because it
    #    prevents a `borrow_mut()` that happens later from causing issues.
    # 2. Start threading `&mut JSContext` through to code that calls layout
    #    queries. This is only done partially now and
    #    `layout_reflow_deprecated` is used to identify remaining call sites to
    #    fix. This makes the borrow hazard more obvious at the call site.
    # 
    # Testing: I could not make a test for this that ran in a reasonable amount of
    # time. This seems to depend on very particular garbage collection timing.
    # Instead I've tried to verify the correctness of this fix using the Rust type
    # system.
    # Fixes: #49184.
    # 
-https://github.com/servo/servo/pull/42287	(@RichardTjokroutomo, #42287)	fonts: Use system APIs to handle font matching on Android platform (#42287)
    ^ commit 5a6a8e169eff752f3be604f5f52e8c1c380c9c11
    # Fonts: Use Android's native APIs to handle font matching. Specifically, this PR made two changes:
    # 
    # - Use Android's `SystemFontIterator` to generate `FontList`. Previously, this was done by parsing the `fonts.xml` file. However, this approach is faulty because Servo currently tries to obtain the family name, but this attribute is not available in fallback families ([link to discussion](https://github.com/servo/servo/issues/33322#issuecomment-2336785375)). Moreover, `fonts.xml` is deprecated on newer releases ([reference](https://cs.android.com/android/platform/superproject/+/android15-release:frameworks/base/data/fonts/fonts.xml)), so it is not a good idea to use this file to create a complete list of system fonts available.
    # - Use Android's `AFontMatcher` to find a fallback font using system API. This builds on [#42776](https://github.com/servo/servo/pull/42776) which made the implementation (finding fallback font using system API) for MacOS.
    # 
    # Testing: Open any websites with non-latin characters on Android (e.g. [Japanese Wikipedia](ja.wikipedia.org)).
    # 
    # Fixes: [#33322](https://github.com/servo/servo/issues/33322) and part of [#41426](https://github.com/servo/servo/issues/41426)
    #

.https://github.com/servo/servo/pull/49230	(@mrobinson, #49230)	script: Do not try to scroll discarded browsing contexts (#49230)
    stability; crash fix
    ^ commit 683a5643514436fd21c60d8e11db4a9afb3364c0
    ^ /!\ contains changes to WPT expectations! it probably affects the web platform
    # When recursively scrolling for `scrollIntoView` do not try to scroll
    # discarded browsing contexts, as they are no longer part of the active
    # page content. This prevents a class of infinite loops where we scroll a
    # discarded browsing context whose `<iframe>` has been adopted by its own
    # document.
    # 
    # This is accomplished by having `WindowProxy::frame_element` return
    # `None` when the browsing context is discarded.
    # 
    # Testing: This change adds a WPT crash test and fixes two WPT subtests.
    # Fixes: #49221.
    #

    # Change from using the `aws-lc-rs` crate to using the `pbkdf2` crate for PBKDF2 support in our WebCrypto API.
    # 
    # Note that, without compilation optimization in development mode, the PBKDF2 implementation in the `pbkdf2` crate could be very slow, and causes timeout in relevant WPT tests. Therefore, raise the optimization level of `sha1`, `sha2` and `script_webcrypto`, which helps prevent false negative WPT results in development mode.
    # 
    # Testing: Refactoring. Existing tests suffice.
    # Part-of: #49215

    # Bumps [libc](https://github.com/rust-lang/libc) from 0.2.190 to 0.2.191.
    # <details>
    # <summary>Release notes</summary>
    # <p><em>Sourced from <a href="https://github.com/rust-lang/libc/releases">libc's releases</a>.</em></p>
    # <blockquote>
    # <h2>0.2.191</h2>
    # <h3>Support</h3>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Emscripten: Support v4.0.12 to v6.0.11 (<a href="https://redirect.github.com/rust-lang/libc/pull/5610">#5610</a>)</li>
    # </ul>
    # <h3>Added</h3>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Apple: Add <code>F_SETNOSIGPIPE</code> and <code>F_GETNOSIGPIPE</code> (<a href="https://redirect.github.com/rust-lang/libc/pull/5609">#5609</a>)</li>
    # <li>Apple: add <code>ifaliasreq</code> and <code>in6_aliasreq</code> (<a href="https://redirect.github.com/rust-lang/libc/pull/5557">#5557</a>)</li>
    # <li>FreeBSD: Add <code>PT_*</code> constants (<a href="https://redirect.github.com/rust-lang/libc/pull/5625">#5625</a>)</li>
    # <li>Haiku: Add <code>AI_*</code> and <code>NI_*</code> constants from netdb.h (<a href="https://redirect.github.com/rust-lang/libc/pull/5585">#5585</a>)</li>
    # <li>Hurd: Add <code>POSIX_SPAWN_*</code> flags (<a href="https://redirect.github.com/rust-lang/libc/pull/5578">#5578</a>)</li>
    # <li>NuttX: Add <code>AT_SYMLINK_NOFOLLOW</code> (<a href="https://redirect.github.com/rust-lang/libc/pull/5614">#5614</a>)</li>
    # <li>OpenBSD: Add <code>sensordev</code> (<a href="https://redirect.github.com/rust-lang/libc/pull/5534">#5534</a>)</li>
    # <li>QuRT: Add <code>isatty</code> (<a href="https://redirect.github.com/rust-lang/libc/pull/5618">#5618</a>)</li>
    # <li>WASI: Add <code>pthread_condattr_{get,set}clock</code> (<a href="https://redirect.github.com/rust-lang/libc/pull/5626">#5626</a>)</li>
    # </ul>
    # <h3>Fixed</h3>
    # <ul>
    # <li>FreeBSD-aarch64: Fix <code>gpregs</code> and <code>fpregs</code> (<a href="https://redirect.github.com/rust-lang/libc/pull/5603">#5603</a>)</li>
    # </ul>
    # <h3>Changed</h3>
    # <ul>
    # <li>AIX: Restore 'c_ulong' as the type of 'statvfs.f_fsid'. (<a href="https://redirect.github.com/rust-lang/libc/pull/5616">#5616</a>)</li>
    # <li>Android: Make <code>prop_info</code> an extern type (<a href="https://redirect.github.com/rust-lang/libc/pull/5561">#5561</a>)</li>
    # <li>Apple: Mach API is now available on non-MacOS targets again. This should resolve some issues building ecosystem crates. (<a href="https://redirect.github.com/rust-lang/libc/pulls/5612">#5612</a>), (<a href="https://redirect.github.com/rust-lang/libc/pull/5636">#5636</a>)</li>
    # <li>FreeBSD: Add back <code>NOTE_PCTRLMASK</code> with a deprecation warning (<a href="https://redirect.github.com/rust-lang/libc/pull/5600">#5600</a>)</li>
    # </ul>
    # </blockquote>

+https://github.com/servo/servo/pull/49239	(@Loirooriol, #49239)	layout: Floor stretch size by zero in block layout heuristics (#49239)
    layout; bugfix
    ^ commit 8665f96a634dc6a9f2406e1d3d1b94c6bea0e323
    ^ /!\ contains changes to WPT expectations! it probably affects the web platform
    # Block layout uses some heuristics to guess whether margins are separated by clearance and then don't collapse. These heuristics weren't flooring the stretch size in the inline axis by zero. Then the logic could end up thinking that the element had a negative width, and then resolve the percentages of its children incorrectly.
    # 
    # Testing: Adding a test
    # 
+https://github.com/servo/servo/pull/49237	(@Loirooriol, #49237)	layout: Avoid negative size when stretching element that avoids floats (#49237)
    layout; bugfix
    ^ commit 20c711d2f1caabeceb803a10c7a5d7cc70992f00
    ^ /!\ contains changes to WPT expectations! it probably affects the web platform
    # Typically, `PlacementAmongFloats` guarantees that the inline size of the placement rect is big enough, but that may fail when dealing with huge sizes that need to be saturated to `MAX_AU`.
    # 
    # It's the same underlying problem as in #37362, and the solution is also flooring by zero.
    # 
    # Testing: Adding test
    # Fixes: #49235
    # 
.https://github.com/servo/servo/pull/49258	(@yezhizhen, #49258)	script/net: Avoid intermediate String when appending to String (#49258)
    perf; allocations
    ^ commit 6b73eb3cdb3f07b9b4acc9fa6e30f28127515e04
    ^ /!\ contains libservo or embedder_traits changes! does it affect our public API?
    # `push_str` only requires a slice. There are a few usages which allocates a String, similar to https://github.com/servo/servo/pull/49219
    # 
    # Testing: It builds.
-https://github.com/servo/servo/pull/49265	(@veyndan, #49265)	android: Remove unusable media support (#49265)
    ^ commit dc11d2eea182f97b265d059a8067d6d2c948e625
    ^ /!\ contains servoshell changes! does it affect the user experience?
    # [As discussed on Zulip](https://servo.zulipchat.com/#narrow/channel/263398-general/topic/Running.20GStreamer.20on.20Android/near/634668458), GStreamer support for Android has been disabled since https://github.com/servo/servo/pull/31086, meaning that all media code in native Android is unused. This is being removed to unblock future refactoring.

    ^ commit 8f064d183f3b4572c99d57db1e020cdeb0f41ab6
    # replace the uses of `handle_potential_webgl_error!` in `WebGLRenderingContext`, with plain Rust. The methods covered are `ActiveTexture`, `BlendEquation`, `BlendEquationSeparate`, `AttachShader`, `DetachShader`, `BindAttribLocation`, `BindFramebuffer`, `BindRenderbuffer`, `BindTexture`, `GenerateMipmap`, `BufferData_`, `BufferData` and `BufferSubData`

    ^ commit e89dcffa3ddc093e2617e5851219ed6f16de6db4

    # This makes `texImage3D` validate `level` before using it to compute the size limits, and applies `MAX_TEXTURE_SIZE` and `MAX_ARRAY_TEXTURE_LAYERS` to `TEXTURE_2D_ARRAY` textures, as required by [OpenGL ES 3.0 3.8.3](https://registry.khronos.org/OpenGL/specs/es/3.0/es_spec_3.0.pdf#nameddest=subsection.3.8.3).

    #

-https://github.com/servo/servo/pull/49307	(@Loirooriol, #49307)	layout: Add methods for inline&block stretch sizes of containing blocks (#49307)
    ^ commit 4320b9aa43f360b44d81735f8f960912e90ea329
    # This introduces the `inline_stretch_size()` and `block_stretch_size()` methods to `ContainingBlock`, and also `block_stretch_size()` to `IndefiniteContainingBlock`. Avoiding the need to repeat the logic everywhere should help avoiding bugs: forgetting to floor by zero was a common occurrence.
    # 
    # Also renames variables for consistency, and changes code to assume that a stretch size is always `Option<Au>`, even in the inline axis.
    # 
    # Testing: No behavior change is intended. There is one occurrence in flexbox where we weren't flooring the stretch size by zero and now we will, but I wasn't able to create a testcase exposing the difference.
    # 
-https://github.com/servo/servo/pull/49200	(@Narfinger, #49200)	script: Introduce `owner_window_unrooted` on `NodeTraits` (#49200)
    ^ commit 46edc9eb2977a12e64bb2a177ff720dc4d2da3f9
    # We introduce `owner_window_unrooted` (similar to document_unrooted`) and use it.
    # This uses the standard techniques of UnrootedDom.
    # 
    # This should reduce rooting for `attribute_mutated` by 0.1% over a complete speedometer 3.1 run.
    # Additionally, we use `onwer_window_unrooted` in a couple of other places because they were easy to transform at first glance.
    # 
    # 
    # Testing: This should not change functionality, just performance characteristics as UnrootedDom enforces rooting correctness.
    #

    # Bumps [cc](https://github.com/rust-lang/cc-rs) from 1.7.0 to 1.9.0.
    # <details>
    # <summary>Release notes</summary>
    # <p><em>Sourced from <a href="https://github.com/rust-lang/cc-rs/releases">cc's releases</a>.</em></p>
    # <blockquote>
    # <h2>cc-v1.9.0</h2>
    # <h3>Added</h3>
    # <ul>
    # <li>pass AddressSanitizer on to the C compiler (<a href="https://redirect.github.com/rust-lang/cc-rs/pull/2045">#2045</a>)</li>
    # <li>add source root for stable external object names (<a href="https://redirect.github.com/rust-lang/cc-rs/pull/2034">#2034</a>)</li>
    # <li>emit rerun-if-env-changed for the compiler's own env vars (<a href="https://redirect.github.com/rust-lang/cc-rs/pull/2035">#2035</a>)</li>
    # <li><em>(env)</em> read Cargo's variables from the env snapshot (<a href="https://redirect.github.com/rust-lang/cc-rs/pull/2028">#2028</a>)</li>
    # <li>collect compile commands into a <code>compile_commands.json</code> (<a href="https://redirect.github.com/rust-lang/cc-rs/pull/1990">#1990</a>)</li>
    # </ul>
    # <h3>Fixed</h3>
    # <ul>
    # <li>don't prefer clang for -Clinker-plugin-lto=no (<a href="https://redirect.github.com/rust-lang/cc-rs/pull/2046">#2046</a>)</li>
    # <li>keep the order of flag and flag_if_supported (<a href="https://redirect.github.com/rust-lang/cc-rs/pull/2044">#2044</a>)</li>
    # <li>correct &quot;arbitary&quot; typo in target parser comment (<a href="https://redirect.github.com/rust-lang/cc-rs/pull/2039">#2039</a>)</li>
    # <li>correct &quot;stablised&quot; typos in TODO comments (<a href="https://redirect.github.com/rust-lang/cc-rs/pull/2040">#2040</a>)</li>
    # <li><em>(msvc)</em> pass objects to the archiver relative to the output directory (<a href="https://redirect.github.com/rust-lang/cc-rs/pull/2033">#2033</a>)</li>
    # <li>honor disabled linker-plugin LTO (<a href="https://redirect.github.com/rust-lang/cc-rs/pull/2024">#2024</a>)</li>
    # <li>apply <code>RUSTC_WRAPPER</code> to the <code>cl.exe</code> cc finds itself (<a href="https://redirect.github.com/rust-lang/cc-rs/pull/2022">#2022</a>)</li>
    # </ul>
    # <h3>Other</h3>

    # Bumps [multiversion](https://github.com/calebzulawski/multiversion) from 0.9.0 to 0.9.1.
    # <details>
    # <summary>Release notes</summary>
    # <p><em>Sourced from <a href="https://github.com/calebzulawski/multiversion/releases">multiversion's releases</a>.</em></p>
    # <blockquote>
    # <h2>0.9.1</h2>
    # <h3>Fixed</h3>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Corrected the minimum <code>rustversion</code> dependency to 1.0.6 to fix builds using minimal dependency versions.</li>
    # <li>Runtime dispatch now skips AVX-512 targets on Intel CPUs without VBMI2 to avoid older implementations' frequency penalties (<a href="https://redirect.github.com/calebzulawski/multiversion/issues/59">#59</a>).</li>
    # </ul>
    # <p><strong>Full Changelog</strong>: <a href="https://github.com/calebzulawski/multiversion/compare/0.9.0...0.9.1">https://github.com/calebzulawski/multiversion/compare/0.9.0...0.9.1</a></p>
    # </blockquote>
    # </details>
    # <details>
    # <summary>Changelog</summary>
    # <p><em>Sourced from <a href="https://github.com/calebzulawski/multiversion/blob/master/CHANGELOG.md">multiversion's changelog</a>.</em></p>
    # <blockquote>
    # <h2>[0.9.1] - 2026-10-20</h2>
    # <h3>Fixed</h3>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Corrected the minimum <code>rustversion</code> dependency to 1.0.6 to fix builds using minimal dependency versions.</li>
    # <li>Runtime dispatch now skips AVX-512 targets on Intel CPUs without VBMI2 to avoid older implementations' frequency penalties (<a href="https://redirect.github.com/calebzulawski/multiversion/issues/59">#59</a>).</li>
    # </ul>
    # </blockquote>
    # </details>
    # <details>
    # <summary>Commits</summary>

    # <details>
    # <summary>Release notes</summary>
    # <p><em>Sourced from <a href="https://github.com/calebzulawski/multiversion/releases">multiversion-macros's releases</a>.</em></p>
    # <blockquote>
    # <h2>0.9.1</h2>
    # <h3>Fixed</h3>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Corrected the minimum <code>rustversion</code> dependency to 1.0.6 to fix builds using minimal dependency versions.</li>
    # <li>Runtime dispatch now skips AVX-512 targets on Intel CPUs without VBMI2 to avoid older implementations' frequency penalties (<a href="https://redirect.github.com/calebzulawski/multiversion/issues/59">#59</a>).</li>
    # </ul>
    # <p><strong>Full Changelog</strong>: <a href="https://github.com/calebzulawski/multiversion/compare/0.9.0...0.9.1">https://github.com/calebzulawski/multiversion/compare/0.9.0...0.9.1</a></p>

    # <blockquote>
    # <h2>[0.9.1] - 2026-10-20</h2>
    # <h3>Fixed</h3>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Corrected the minimum <code>rustversion</code> dependency to 1.0.6 to fix builds using minimal dependency versions.</li>
    # <li>Runtime dispatch now skips AVX-512 targets on Intel CPUs without VBMI2 to avoid older implementations' frequency penalties (<a href="https://redirect.github.com/calebzulawski/multiversion/issues/59">#59</a>).</li>
    # </ul>
    # </blockquote>

.https://github.com/servo/servo/pull/49207	(@gabrielecabrini, #49207)	constellation: Reply to WebGPU requests from opaque origins (#49207)
    stability; crash fix
    ^ commit 36d88bca2ae6307e4739b55f3f9f4a8bae2ea16e
    # `handle_wgpu_request` returned early when the source pipeline URL had no
    # registered domain (e.g. `data:` URLs or `about:srcdoc` iframes), dropping
    # the response sender without replying. Script then panicked in
    # `HTMLCanvasElement::get_or_init_webgpu_context` because `recv()` returned
    # `Disconnected`. Now the constellation replies with `None`, so
    # `getContext("webgpu")` returns `null` instead of crashing.
    # 
    # Testing: Added webgpu-opaque-origin-crash.html
    # Fixes: #49197
-https://github.com/servo/servo/pull/49306	(@mrobinson, #49306)	servoshell: Switch to `rfd` for file selection dialogs (#49306)
    ^ commit 7210ba1f2c1b0ebdd6d7632277aaa780114874de
    ^ /!\ contains servoshell changes! does it affect the user experience?
    # This switches servoshell's dialog implementation from `egui-file-dialog`
    # to `rfd` which shows native system file selection dialogs. Reasoning:
    # 
    # 1. Native file dialogs are more natural to show on a desktop platform.
    # 2. `egui-file-dialog` depends on a specific `egui` version so having it
    #    in the list of dependencies sometimes holds up `egui` upgrades.
    # 
    # In order to do this we must no longer hide file dialog embedder controls
    # when the file input loses focus. File selection dialogs are often modal
    # and show in a separate top-level window which can trigger a `blur`
    # event. Triggering a hide here means the file dialog result would be
    # thrown away.
    # 
    # Testing: There is no testing for this part of servoshell.
    # Fixes: #49170.
    # 
    # 
.https://github.com/servo/servo/pull/49313	(@yezhizhen, #49313)	net: Avoid cloning headers twice when filtering response (#49313)
    perf; allocations
    ^ commit daaf1697b8cc3580c98f18d7b233598fd0601bd0
    # We can drain the headers in response before cloning, and then move it back in the end, since we've already been crafting new headers in every branch later with old ones discarded earlier.
    # 
    # Testing: It builds.

.https://github.com/servo/servo/pull/49374	(@mrobinson, #49374)	script: No longer rely on the incumbent global when getting JSON attributes (#49374)
    stability; crash fix
    ^ commit 1c7330c14ace176afecf61b2d262295edded4494
    ^ /!\ contains changes to WPT expectations! it probably affects the web platform
    # The included test case panicked because it triggered an unhandled
    # promise rejection. The stringifier would run as part of that code path
    # without an incumbent global. This change fixes that in two places:
    # 
    # 1. Stringification in this case is wrapped in `run_a_script` so that the
    #    settings stack is never empty. Stringification can run code, so this
    #    is important.
    # 2. Instead of getting the incumbent global for `CollectJSONAttributes`,
    #    the global is fetched from the object using the SpiderMonkey
    #    API. This more closely matches Firefox.
    # 
    # Testing: This change adds a WPT crash test.
    # Fixes: #49308.
    #

    ^ commit 1e5431f76f884dc12d8340c5128442529e2132b5
    # Bumps [fixed_decimal](https://github.com/unicode-org/icu4x) from 0.7.2 to 0.7.3.
    # <details>
    # <summary>Changelog</summary>
    # <p><em>Sourced from <a href="https://github.com/unicode-org/icu4x/blob/main/CHANGELOG.md">fixed_decimal's changelog</a>.</em></p>
    # <blockquote>
    # <h1>Changelog</h1>
    # <h2>icu4x 2.4</h2>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Components
    # <ul>
    # <li>General
    # <ul>
    # <li>Updated to Unicode 18</li>
    # <li>Updated to CLDR 49</li>
    # <li>Updated to TZDB 2026e</li>
    # </ul>
    # </li>
    # <li><code>icu_codepointtrie_builder</code>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Retired (unicode-org#8540)</li>
    # </ul>
    # </li>
    # <li><code>icu_collator</code>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Retain trie value for Hangul syllables in <code>push_decomposed_starter</code> (unicode-org#8484)</li>
    # <li>Don't output a trailing <code>NO_CE</code> (0x01) byte when generating sort keys in order to match ICU4C's pre-existing behavior.  (unicode-org#8536)
    # <ul>
    # <li>⚠️ This changes sort key output incompatibly!</li>
    # </ul>
    # </li>
    # <li>Fix human-readable serde deserialization of <code>CollationSpecialPrimaries</code> (unicode-org#8551)</li>
    # </ul>
    # </li>
    # <li><code>icu_collections</code>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Fix a bug in <code>CodePointInversionList::iter_ranges_complemented</code> when the last range includes U+10FFFF (unicode-org#8456)</li>
    # <li>Add <code>CodePointTrie::null_value()</code> accessor (unicode-org#8544)</li>
    # <li>Add <code>Char16Trie::cursor</code> API, deprecating <code>Char16Trie::iter</code> (which was not an <code>Iterator</code>) (unicode-org#8547)</li>
    # <li>New <code>icu_collections::codepointtrie::CodePointTrieBuilder</code> (unicode-org#8540)</li>
    # <li>Add <code>Char16Trie::iter2</code> to iterate over key-value pairs (unicode-org#8548)</li>
    # </ul>
    # </li>
    # <li><code>icu_datetime</code>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Reduce ambiguity in Medium and Long formats for <code>fieldsets::MD</code> and <code>fieldsets::MDE</code> (unicode-org#8447)</li>
    # <li>implement source parts for <code>DateRangeFormatter</code> (unicode-org#8448)
    # <ul>
    # <li>New items: <code>parts::RANGE_START</code> and <code>parts::RANGE_END</code></li>
    # </ul>
    # </li>
    # <li>(unstable) New module <code>fieldbag</code>, struct <code>DateTimeFieldBag</code>, and related enums for expressing field-by-field models for datetime formats. (unicode-org#8503)
    # <ul>
    # <li>Conversion to and from field sets (unicode-org#8517)</li>
    # <li>Round-trip to UTS 35 skeleton strings (unicode-org#8527)</li>
    # </ul>
    # </li>
    # <li>Add <code>VALUES</code> associated constants to <code>options</code> enums (unicode-org#8532)
    # <ul>
    # <li>New associated constants in <code>icu_datetime::options</code>: <code>Length::VALUES</code>, <code>Alignment::VALUES</code>, <code>YearStyle::VALUES</code>, <code>TimePrecision::VALUES</code>, <code>SubsecondDigits::VALUES</code></li>
    # </ul>
    # </li>
    # <li>Implement fallback to single-value formatting when there is no diff during range formatting (unicode-org#8480)</li>
    # </ul>
    # </li>
    # <li><code>icu_experimental</code>: <code>0.6.0 -&gt; 0.7.0</code>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Renamed data marker <code>UnitIdsV1</code> to <code>UnitsIdV1</code>. (unicode-org#8473)</li>
    # <li>Added <code>CurrencyDecimalSymbolsV1</code> data marker for per-currency decimal symbol overrides. (unicode-org#8463)</li>
    # <li>Currency formatting uses the no-currency pattern when CLDR defines no symbol for a currency in a locale (e.g. <code>PTE</code> in <code>pt-PT</code>) (unicode-org#8485)</li>
    # <li><code>RuleCollection::register_source</code> now takes the source by reference (unicode-org#8597)</li>
    # </ul>
    # </li>
    # <li><code>icu_normalizer</code>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Fix soundness bug around <code>split_normalized_utf8</code>, <code>normalize_utf8</code>. (unicode-org#8628)</li>
    # </ul>
    # </li>
    # <li><code>icu_pattern</code>: <code>0.5.0 -&gt; 0.5.1</code>
    # <ul>
    # <li>No code changes, re-released to pick up the latest ICU4X dependency versions</li>
    # </ul>
    # </li>
    # <li><code>icu_properties</code>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Add <code>Block</code> enumerated property (unicode-org#8409)</li>
    # <li>Fix UnicodeSet parser to support surrogate code points in escape sequences (unicode-org#8507)
    # <ul>
    # <li>Escape sequences like <code>\uD800</code>, <code>\x{D800}</code>, and <code>\U0000D800</code> now parse correctly</li>
    # <li>Surrogates in string contexts (e.g., <code>{\uD800}</code>) are still rejected</li>
    # </ul>
    # </li>
    # </ul>
    # </li>
    # <li><code>icu_segmenter</code>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Performance improvements for V1/V3 complex word and line segmenters. (unicode-org#8144)</li>
    # <li>Fix multiple bugs in the dictionary segmenter (unicode-org#8523, unicode-org#8611)</li>
    # <li>Fix line breaks at the end of Southeast Asian (SA) runs in the V1/V3 line segmenters (unicode-org#8574)</li>
    # </ul>
    # </li>
    # </ul>
    # </li>
    # </ul>
    # <!-- raw HTML omitted -->

    # Bumps [memmap2](https://github.com/RazrFalcon/memmap2-rs) from 0.9.11 to 0.9.12.
    # <details>
    # <summary>Changelog</summary>
    # <p><em>Sourced from <a href="https://github.com/RazrFalcon/memmap2-rs/blob/master/CHANGELOG.md">memmap2's changelog</a>.</em></p>
    # <blockquote>
    # <h2>[0.9.12] - 2026-10-21</h2>
    # <h3>Added</h3>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Add <code>MmapOptions::map_if_safe()</code> to allow creation read-only mappings of a file from safe code if the kernel can guarantee the file is immutable and large enough.
    # <a href="https://github.com/alexlarsson"><code>@​alexlarsson</code></a></li>
    # <li>Add <code>MmapOptions::no_probe_handle()</code> to disable probing of file handles on Windows to improve performance, at the cost of no longer being able to remap later with more capabilities.
    # <a href="https://github.com/mat-gas"><code>@​mat-gas</code></a></li>
    # <li>Add support for OpenHarmony targets (<code>*-unknown-linux-ohos</code>).
    # <a href="https://github.com/hiyms"><code>@​hiyms</code></a></li>
    # </ul>
    # <h3>Changed</h3>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Cache allocation granularity on Windows to prevent repeated syscalls.
    # <a href="https://github.com/mat-gas"><code>@​mat-gas</code></a></li>
    # </ul>
    # </blockquote>
    # </details>
    # <details>

+https://github.com/servo/servo/pull/49174	(@mrobinson, #49174)	layout: Do not combine perspective with transform matrix (#49174)
    layout; bugfix
    ^ commit 6b285dee6ecfa69aee016e1d48a1d0c814009c44
    ^ /!\ contains changes to WPT expectations! it probably affects the web platform
    # Previously Servo was multiplying a box's perspective with its transform
    # matrix to make a single spatial node. This is wrong because perspective
    # should only apply to children and not to the box itself. This change
    # makes it so that perspective creates a separate node for children.

    ^ commit 988d63c306a3688229c4df45ea926f4ea57808cc
    # The updated WebRender backports fixes for a regression, where on high DPI displays servo would draw color bitmap emoji at 1/DPR of the intended size. The same could also be observed when applying `transform: scale()` to emoji.
    # 
    # The issue manifested slightly differently in Gecko, since Gecko apparently builds display lists in device pixels. Servo however applies a scale transform to get to device pixels (https://github.com/servo/servo/blob/6d3c9c30951d019d997e364fee133669ba234679/components/paint/painter.rs#L674)
    # 
    # Testing: Testing DPR is not possible with the current servo testrunner. Adding a reftest that uses scale might be possible, but since the regression was covered by a wrench test in WebRender, I didn't try to add one.
    #

.https://github.com/servo/servo/pull/43561	(@stevennovaryo, @mrobinson, #43561)	paint: Update pinch zoom transformation using dynamic WebRender property (#43561)
    perf; less work
    ^ commit 148cec920975b36f26207a161aba825f98a1f3bd
    # To handle PinchZoom and other scaling like HiDPI or page zoom, we are using display list reference frame that stores such scale and translation. Because of this, currently we are rebuilding the display list each time we need to update such zoom. For pinch zoom's perspective this would be costly because pinch zoom itself doesn't affects the layout. 
    # 
    # Therefore following the changes to caret animation, this PR uses WebRender `PropertyBinding` to optimize such cases. Where we are sending the `PropertyBinding` transforms update instead.
    # 
    # Testing: Manual test with `/visual-viewport/` WPTs.
    #

+https://github.com/servo/servo/pull/48793	(@TimvdLippe, @mrobinson, #48793)	net: Do not unconditionally overwrite origin when handling CORS (#48793)
    net; bugfix for fetch metadata
    ^ commit 4dfd2701b8aaa24f7c536b1be41cacb60ccd8786
    ^ /!\ contains changes to WPT expectations! it probably affects the web platform
    # When the cors_flag would be set, we would always set the origin back to an opaque origin. This causes same-site and cross-site computation to break, since we would incorrectly reset the origin back to an opaque for perfectly fine origins.
    # 
    # Instead, remove that step (which also wasn't in the spec) and rewrite the logic to only perform when the request mode is actually Cors.
    # 
    # Closes #49262 (no longer required)
    # Closes #49263 (now obsolete)
    # 
    # Testing: most fetch metadata tests now pass

It loads an `<audio>` from a blob URL and has three subtests: the URL is never revoked, it is revoked in a microtask after the fetch has started (both should load), and it is revoked before `src` is set (should fire `error` with `MEDIA_ERR_SRC_NOT_SUPPORTED`). All three pass with the fix. Try run: https://github.com/rogerkorantenng/servo/actions/runs/39981862598
